
clothes = {

	usage = game
	selection_behavior = weighted_random
	fallback = default_clothes

	no_clothes = {
        dna_modifiers = {
            accessory = {
                mode = add
                gene = clothes
                template = no_clothes
                range = { 0 1 } # For the randomness to work correctly
            }
        }   
        outfit_tags = { no_clothes }
        weight = {
            base = 0
            modifier = {
                add = 1000
                should_be_fully_naked_portrait_trigger = yes
            }
            modifier = {
                add = 1000
                should_be_topless_portrait_trigger = yes
            }
			modifier = {
				add = 1000
				POD_portrait_event_naked_trigger = yes
				should_show_nudity = yes
			}			
            modifier = {
            	add = -1000
            	should_show_nudity = no
            }
        }
    }

    nightgown_western = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = clothes
				template = western_sickness
				range = { 0 1 } # For the randomness to work correctly
			}
			morph = {
				mode = modify_multiply
				gene = gene_bs_bust
				value = 0.8 # Setting this lower to reduce the exaggerated effect of large bust size when wearing clothes
				template = bust_clothes
			}

		}
		outfit_tags = { no_clothes }
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 110
				should_show_nudity = no
				should_be_naked_trigger = yes
			}
			modifier = {
            	add = -1000
            	should_show_nudity = yes
            }
            modifier = {
            	add = -50
				portrait_byzantine_clothing_trigger = no
				portrait_northern_clothing_trigger = no
				portrait_western_clothing_trigger = no
				portrait_french_clothing_trigger = no
				portrait_italian_clothing_trigger = no	
				portrait_gaelic_clothing_trigger = no			
				portrait_northern_slav_clothing_trigger = no	
				portrait_balto_finnic_clothing_trigger = no			
				portrait_iberia_clothing_trigger = no
			    portrait_hungarian_clothing_trigger = no	
				portrait_german_clothing_trigger = no	
				portrait_georgian_clothing_trigger = no	
				portrait_bulgarian_clothing_trigger = no
				portrait_armenian_clothing_trigger = no		
				portrait_south_slav_clothing_trigger = no	
				portrait_anglo_saxon_clothing_trigger = no					
            }
		}
	}

	nightgown_mena = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = clothes
				template = mena_sickness
				range = { 0 1 } # For the randomness to work correctly
			}
			morph = {
				mode = modify_multiply

				value = 0.8 # Setting this lower to reduce the exaggerated effect of large bust size when wearing clothes

			}

		}	
		outfit_tags = { no_clothes }
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 110
				should_show_nudity = no
				should_be_naked_trigger = yes
			}
			modifier = {
            	add = -1000
            	should_show_nudity = yes
            }
            modifier = {
				add = 1100
				exists = this
				has_character_modifier = vow_of_poverty_modifier
			}
            modifier = {
            	add = -50
				portrait_mena_clothing_trigger = no
				portrait_indian_clothing_trigger = no
				portrait_steppe_clothing_trigger = no
				portrait_african_clothing_trigger = no
				#portrait_ghaznavid_clothing_trigger = no				
				portrait_seljuk_clothing_trigger = no
				portrait_dde_abbasid_clothing_trigger = no	
				portrait_iranian_clothing_trigger = no		
				portrait_south_indian_clothing_trigger = no		
				portrait_east_african_clothing_trigger = no		
				portrait_early_eastern_iranian_trigger = no	
				portrait_cuman_clothing_trigger = no	
				portrait_uyghur_clothing_trigger = no	
				portrait_iberia_muslim_clothing_trigger = no	
				portrait_maghreb_clothing_trigger = no	
			    portrait_Khazar_clothing_trigger = no	
			    portrait_kirghiz_clothing_trigger = no
				portrait_burmese_clothing_trigger = no		
			    portrait_tibetan_clothing_trigger = no	
				portrait_western_turk_clothing_trigger = no	
				portrait_magyar_clothing_trigger = no					
            }
		}
	}

    poverty_robes_western = {
    	dna_modifiers = {
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = clothes
				template = western_sickness
				range = { 0 1 } # For the randomness to work correctly
			}
			morph = {
				mode = modify_multiply
				gene = gene_bs_bust
				value = 0.8 # Setting this lower to reduce the exaggerated effect of large bust size when wearing clothes
				template = bust_clothes
			}

		}
		weight = {
			base = 0
			# Boring clothes for characters with an active Vow of Poverty
			modifier = {
				add = 100
				exists = this
				has_character_modifier = vow_of_poverty_modifier
				# Should still wear armor when going to war
				NOT = { portrait_wear_armor_trigger = yes }
			}
			modifier = {
            	add = -50
				portrait_byzantine_clothing_trigger = no
				portrait_northern_clothing_trigger = no
				portrait_western_clothing_trigger = no
				portrait_french_clothing_trigger = no

## So that the Prophet Muhammad does not have a portrait ## 

	no_portrait = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			accessory = {
				mode = add				
				gene = clothes
				template = no_clothes
				value = 0.5
			}
		}
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 2000
				exists = this
				this = character:33922
			}
		}
	}

## Small children ## 

	western_children = {

		dna_modifiers = {
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = clothes
				template = western_children_commoner_clothes
				range = { 0 1 } # For the randomness to work correctly
			}
			morph = {
				mode = modify_multiply
				gene = gene_bs_bust
				value = 0.8 # Setting this lower to reduce the exaggerated effect of large bust size when wearing clothes
				template = bust_clothes
			}

		}	
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 100
				scope:age < 4
			}
		}
	}


## DDE HRE ##

	dde_hre_commoners = {

		dna_modifiers = {
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = clothes
				template = dde_hre_commoner_clothes
				range = { 0 1 } # For the randomness to work correctly
			}
			morph = {
				mode = modify_multiply
				gene = gene_bs_bust
				value = 0.8 # Setting this lower to reduce the exaggerated effect of large bust size when wearing clothes
				template = bust_clothes
			}

		}	
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 20
				scope:highest_held_title_tier < tier_county
				trigger_if = {
					limit = { exists = this }
					highest_held_title_tier < tier_county
					NOR = {
						AND = {
							exists = primary_spouse
							primary_spouse = {
								highest_held_title_tier > tier_county
							}
						}
						AND = {
							has_government = republic_government
							primary_title.tier > 1
						}
						AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
					}
				}
				portrait_dde_hre_clothing_trigger = yes
				#portrait_western_christian_trigger = yes
			}
			# ## Exclude characters with the has_scripted_clothes flag, mostly used for developer characters
   #          modifier = {
   #              factor = 0
   #              has_character_flag = has_scripted_clothes
   #          }
		}
	}
	
	dde_hre_low_nobles = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = clothes
				template = dde_hre_low_nobility_clothes
				range = { 0 1 } # For the randomness to work correctly
			}
			morph = {
				mode = modify_multiply
				gene = gene_bs_bust
				value = 0.8 # Setting this lower to reduce the exaggerated effect of large bust size when wearing clothes
				template = bust_clothes
			}

		}	
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				trigger_if = {
					limit = { exists = this }
					OR = {
						AND = {
							highest_held_title_tier = tier_county
							NOR = {
								# has_dynasty = no
								AND = {
									has_government = republic_government
									primary_title.tier > 1
								}
								AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
							}
							portrait_dde_hre_clothing_trigger = yes
							#portrait_western_christian_trigger = yes
						}
						AND = {
							exists = primary_spouse
							OR = {
								is_ruler = no
								highest_held_title_tier = tier_barony
							}
							primary_spouse = {
								highest_held_title_tier = tier_county
								NOR = {
									# has_dynasty = no
									AND = {
										has_government = republic_government
										primary_title.tier > 1
									}
									AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
								}
								portrait_dde_hre_clothing_spouse_trigger = yes
								#portrait_western_christian_trigger = yes
							}
						}
					}
				}
				trigger_else = {
					portrait_dde_hre_clothing_trigger = yes
					#portrait_western_christian_trigger = yes
					scope:highest_held_title_tier = tier_county
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				exists = court_owner
				court_owner = {
					has_royal_court = yes
					amenity_level = { type = court_fashion value >= 4 }
				}
				portrait_dde_hre_clothing_trigger = yes
			}
		}
	}

	dde_hre_high_nobles = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = clothes
				template = dde_hre_high_nobility_clothes
				range = { 0 1 } # For the randomness to work correctly
			}
			morph = {
				mode = modify_multiply
				gene = gene_bs_bust
				value = 0.8 # Setting this lower to reduce the exaggerated effect of large bust size when wearing clothes
				template = bust_clothes
			}

		}	
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				exists = court_owner
				court_owner = {
					has_royal_court = yes
					amenity_level = { type = court_fashion value = 5 }
				}
				portrait_dde_hre_clothing_trigger = yes
				#portrait_western_christian_trigger = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				trigger_if = {
					limit = { exists = this }
					OR = {
						AND = {
							OR = {
								highest_held_title_tier = tier_duchy
								AND = {
									has_government = republic_government
									primary_title.tier > 1
								}
								AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
							}
							NOT = { AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = mercenary_government  } } # Blocked for mercenaries
							portrait_dde_hre_clothing_trigger = yes
							#portrait_western_christian_trigger = yes
						}
						AND = {
							exists = primary_spouse
							OR = {
								is_ruler = no
								highest_held_title_tier = tier_barony
							}
							primary_spouse = {
								OR = {
									highest_held_title_tier = tier_duchy
									AND = {
										has_government = republic_government
										primary_title.tier > 1
									}
									AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
								}
								portrait_dde_hre_clothing_spouse_trigger = yes
								#portrait_western_christian_trigger = yes
							}
						}
					}
					
				}
				trigger_else = {
					scope:highest_held_title_tier = tier_duchy
					portrait_dde_hre_clothing_trigger = yes
					#portrait_western_christian_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	dde_hre_war = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = clothes
				template = dde_hre_war_nobility_clothes
				range = { 0 1 } # For the randomness to work correctly
			}
			morph = {
				mode = modify_multiply
				gene = gene_bs_bust
				value = 0.8 # Setting this lower to reduce the exaggerated effect of large bust size when wearing clothes
				template = bust_clothes
			}

		}
		outfit_tags = { military_outfit }
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 102		# slightly stronger than western war
				portrait_wear_armor_trigger = yes
				portrait_dde_hre_clothing_trigger = yes
			 OR = {
				EPE_armor_era_late_medieval_trigger = yes	
				EPE_armor_era_high_medieval_trigger = yes						
				}				
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 21		# So priests who are actively serving in a raised army as a commander or knight wear armor instead of priest robes
				portrait_wear_armor_currently_in_army_trigger = yes
			 OR = {
				EPE_armor_era_late_medieval_trigger = yes	
				EPE_armor_era_high_medieval_trigger = yes						
				}					
			}
		}
	}

						}
						AND = {
							exists = primary_spouse
							OR = {
								is_ruler = no
								highest_held_title_tier = tier_barony
							}
							primary_spouse = {
								highest_held_title_tier = tier_kingdom
								NOR = {
									AND = {
									has_government = republic_government
										primary_title.tier > 1
									}
									AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
								}
								portrait_western_clothing_spouse_trigger = yes
								#portrait_western_christian_trigger = yes
							}
						}
					}
				}
				trigger_else = {
					scope:highest_held_title_tier = tier_kingdom
					portrait_western_clothing_trigger = yes
					#portrait_western_christian_trigger = yes
					
				}
			}
		}
	}

####Tribal Era Crusader
	tribal_era_western_crusades = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = clothes
				template = tribal_era_western_war
				range = { 0 1 } # For the randomness to work correctly
			}
			morph = {
				mode = modify_multiply
				gene = gene_bs_bust
				value = 0.8 # Setting this lower to reduce the exaggerated effect of large bust size when wearing clothes
				template = bust_clothes
			}

		}
		outfit_tags = { military_outfit }
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 110
				exists = this
				EPE_armor_era_tribal_medieval_trigger = yes						
				OR = {
				    AND = {
				        religion = religion:christianity_religion
				        has_government = holy_order_government
				    }
				    AND = {
				        religion = religion:christianity_religion
				        is_landed = no
				        portrait_wear_armor_trigger = yes
				        exists = liege
				        liege = {
				            has_government = holy_order_government
				        }
				    }
				    AND = {
				        religion = religion:christianity_religion
				        portrait_wear_armor_trigger = yes
				        OR = {
				            any_character_war = {
				                OR = {
				                    using_cb = undirected_great_holy_war
				                    using_cb = directed_great_holy_war
				                }
				                is_attacker = prev
				            }
				            any_liege_or_above = {
				                faith = prev.faith
				                any_character_war = {
				                    OR = {
				                        using_cb = undirected_great_holy_war
				                        using_cb = directed_great_holy_war
				                    }
				                    is_attacker = prev
				                }
				            }
				        }
				    }
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 21		# So priests who are actively serving in a raised army as a commander or knight wear armor instead of priest robes
				EPE_armor_era_tribal_medieval_trigger = yes						
				portrait_wear_armor_currently_in_army_trigger = yes
			}
		}
	}

	byzantine_commoners = {

		dna_modifiers = {
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = clothes
				template = byzantine_commoner_clothes
				range = { 0 1 } # For the randomness to work correctly
			}
			morph = {
				mode = modify_multiply
				gene = gene_bs_bust
				value = 0.8 # Setting this lower to reduce the exaggerated effect of large bust size when wearing clothes
				template = bust_clothes
			}

		}	
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 20
				scope:highest_held_title_tier < tier_county
				trigger_if = {
					limit = { exists = this }
					NOR = {
						AND = {
							exists = primary_spouse
							primary_spouse = {
								highest_held_title_tier > tier_county
							}
						}
						AND = {
							has_government = republic_government
							primary_title.tier > 1
						}
						AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
					}
				}
				portrait_byzantine_clothing_spouse_trigger = yes
			}
		}
	}

								portrait_byzantine_clothing_spouse_trigger = yes
							}
						}
					}
				}
				trigger_else = {
					scope:highest_held_title_tier = tier_empire
					portrait_byzantine_clothing_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}

				portrait_byzantine_clothing_trigger = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 21		# So priests who are actively serving in a raised army as a commander or knight wear armor instead of priest robes
				portrait_wear_armor_currently_in_army_trigger = yes
			}
		}
	}

## INDIAN ##

	indian_commoners = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = clothes
				template = indian_commoner_clothes
				range = { 0 1 } # For the randomness to work correctly
			}
			morph = {
				mode = modify_multiply
				gene = gene_bs_bust
				value = 0.8 # Setting this lower to reduce the exaggerated effect of large bust size when wearing clothes
				template = bust_clothes_light
			}

		}	
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 20
				scope:highest_held_title_tier < tier_county
				trigger_if = {
					limit = { exists = this }
					NOR = {
						AND = {
							exists = primary_spouse
							primary_spouse = {
								highest_held_title_tier > tier_county
							}
						}
						AND = {
							has_government = republic_government
							primary_title.tier > 1
						}
						AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
					}
				}
				portrait_indian_clothing_trigger = yes
			}
		}
	}

	indian_low_nobles = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = clothes
				template = indian_low_nobility_clothes
				range = { 0 1 } # For the randomness to work correctly
			}
			morph = {
				mode = modify_multiply
				gene = gene_bs_bust
				value = 0.8 # Setting this lower to reduce the exaggerated effect of large bust size when wearing clothes
				template = bust_clothes_light
			}

		}	
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				exists = court_owner
				court_owner = {
					has_royal_court = yes
					amenity_level = { type = court_fashion value >= 4 }
				}
				portrait_indian_clothing_trigger = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				trigger_if = {
					limit = { exists = this }
					OR = {
						AND = {
							highest_held_title_tier = tier_county
							NOR = {
								# has_dynasty = no
								AND = {
									has_government = republic_government
									primary_title.tier > 1
								}
								AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
							}
							portrait_indian_clothing_trigger = yes
						}
						AND = {
							exists = primary_spouse
							OR = {
								is_ruler = no
								highest_held_title_tier = tier_barony
							}
							primary_spouse = {
								highest_held_title_tier = tier_county
								NOR = {
									# has_dynasty = no
									AND = {
										has_government = republic_government
										primary_title.tier > 1
									}
									AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
								}
								portrait_indian_clothing_spouse_trigger = yes
							}
						}
					}
				}
				trigger_else = {
					scope:highest_held_title_tier = tier_county
					portrait_indian_clothing_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}

	indian_high_nobles = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = clothes
				template = indian_high_nobility_clothes
				range = { 0 1 } # For the randomness to work correctly
			}
			morph = {
				mode = modify_multiply
				gene = gene_bs_bust
				value = 0.8 # Setting this lower to reduce the exaggerated effect of large bust size when wearing clothes
				template = bust_clothes_light
			}

		}		
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				exists = court_owner
				court_owner = {
					has_royal_court = yes
					amenity_level = { type = court_fashion value = 5 }
				}
				portrait_indian_clothing_trigger = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				trigger_if = {
					limit = { exists = this }
					OR = {
						AND = {
							OR = {
								highest_held_title_tier > tier_county
								AND = {
									has_government = republic_government
									primary_title.tier > 1
								}
								AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
							}
							NOT = { AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = mercenary_government  } } # Blocked for mercenaries
							portrait_indian_clothing_trigger = yes
						}
						AND = {
							exists = primary_spouse
							OR = {
								is_ruler = no
								highest_held_title_tier = tier_barony
							}
							primary_spouse = {
								OR = {
									highest_held_title_tier > tier_county
									AND = {
									has_government = republic_government
										primary_title.tier > 1
									}
									AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
								}
								portrait_indian_clothing_spouse_trigger = yes
							}
						}
					}	
				}
				trigger_else = {
					scope:highest_held_title_tier > tier_county
					portrait_indian_clothing_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}

							has_title = title:d_sunni
						}
						NOT = { AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = mercenary_government  } } # Blocked for mercenaries
				EPE_culture_era_early_medieval_trigger = yes				
					}

							OR = {

									AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
								}

					portrait_mena_clothing_spouse_trigger = yes

                    NOT = { portrait_dde_abbasid_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes } 					
						}					
				}	
							}
						}
					}
				}
				trigger_else = {
					scope:highest_held_title_tier = tier_county
				OR = {
					portrait_mena_clothing_trigger = yes					
					AND = {
					portrait_dde_abbasid_clothing_trigger = yes	
                    NOT = { portrait_dde_abbasid_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes } 					
						}					
				 }
				}

				template = bust_clothes
			}

		}	
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				scope:highest_held_title_tier < tier_county
				trigger_if = {
					limit = { exists = this }
					NOR = {
						AND = {
							exists = primary_spouse
							primary_spouse = {
								highest_held_title_tier > tier_county
							}
						}
						AND = {
							has_government = republic_government
							primary_title.tier > 1
						}
						AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
					}
				}
						OR = {
							AND = {
					        portrait_northern_clothing_trigger = yes	
                            portrait_northern_non_christian_trigger = yes 					
							}
							AND = {
					        portrait_balto_finnic_clothing_trigger = yes	
                            NOT = { portrait_norse_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes } 					
								}
							AND = {
				            portrait_west_slav_clothing_trigger = yes
                            NOT = { portrait_norse_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes }							
		                     ###tribal medieval headgears	
				            OR = {
				            EPE_armor_era_tribal_medieval_trigger = yes	##High to late era			
				            current_date <= 1044.1.1	##early era				
				                  }			
								}								
						}
			}

	northern_high_nobles = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = clothes
				template = northern_high_nobility_clothes
				range = { 0 1 } # For the randomness to work correctly
			}
			morph = {
				mode = modify_multiply
				gene = gene_bs_bust
				value = 0.8 # Setting this lower to reduce the exaggerated effect of large bust size when wearing clothes
				template = bust_clothes
			}

		}	
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				trigger_if = {
					limit = { exists = this }
					OR = {
						AND = {
							OR = {
								highest_held_title_tier = tier_duchy
								AND = {
									has_government = republic_government
									primary_title.tier > 1
								}
								AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
							}
							NOT = { AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = mercenary_government  } } # Blocked for mercenaries
						OR = {
								AND = {
					             portrait_northern_clothing_trigger = yes	
                                 portrait_northern_non_christian_trigger = yes 					
								}
								AND = {
					             portrait_balto_finnic_clothing_trigger = yes	
                                 NOT = { portrait_norse_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes } 					
								}
								AND = {
				            portrait_west_slav_clothing_trigger = yes
                            NOT = { portrait_norse_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes }							
		                     ###tribal medieval headgears	
				             OR = {
				            EPE_armor_era_tribal_medieval_trigger = yes	##High to late era			
				            current_date <= 1044.1.1	##early era				
				                  }			
								}									
						}
						}
						AND = {
							exists = primary_spouse
							OR = {
								is_ruler = no
								highest_held_title_tier = tier_barony
							}
							primary_spouse = {
								OR = {
									highest_held_title_tier = tier_duchy
									AND = {
										has_government = republic_government
										primary_title.tier > 1
									}
									AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
								}
						         OR = {
								  AND = {
					               portrait_northern_clothing_spouse_trigger = yes	
                                   portrait_northern_non_christian_trigger = yes					
								   }
								  AND = {
					               portrait_balto_finnic_clothing_spouse_trigger = yes	
                                   NOT = { portrait_norse_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes } 					
								   }
								AND = {
				            portrait_west_slav_clothing_spouse_trigger = yes
                            NOT = { portrait_norse_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes }							
		                     ###tribal medieval headgears	
				             OR = {
				            EPE_armor_era_tribal_medieval_trigger = yes	##High to late era			
				            current_date <= 1044.1.1	##early era				
				                  }			
								}								   
						        }
							}
						}
					}
				}
				trigger_else = {
					scope:highest_held_title_tier = tier_duchy
						OR = {
							AND = {
					        portrait_northern_clothing_trigger = yes	
                            portrait_northern_non_christian_trigger = yes 					
							}	
							AND = {
					        portrait_balto_finnic_clothing_trigger = yes	
                            NOT = { portrait_norse_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes } 					
								}
							AND = {
				            portrait_west_slav_clothing_trigger = yes
                            NOT = { portrait_norse_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes }							
		                     ###tribal medieval headgears	
				             OR = {
				            EPE_armor_era_tribal_medieval_trigger = yes	##High to late era			
				            current_date <= 1044.1.1	##early era				
				                  }			
								}									
						}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	northern_pagan_royalty = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = clothes
				template = northern_high_nobility_clothes
				range = { 0 1 } # For the randomness to work correctly
			}
			morph = {
				mode = modify_multiply
				gene = gene_bs_bust
				value = 0.8 # Setting this lower to reduce the exaggerated effect of large bust size when wearing clothes
				template = bust_clothes
			}

		}	
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				trigger_if = {
					limit = { exists = this }
					OR = {
						AND = {
							highest_held_title_tier = tier_kingdom
							NOR = {
								AND = {
									has_government = republic_government
									primary_title.tier > 1
								}
								AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
							}
						OR = {
								AND = {
					             portrait_northern_clothing_trigger = yes	
                                 portrait_northern_non_christian_trigger = yes 					
								}
								AND = {
					             portrait_balto_finnic_clothing_trigger = yes	
                                 NOT = { portrait_norse_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes } 					
								}
								AND = {
				                portrait_west_slav_clothing_trigger = yes
                                NOT = { portrait_norse_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes }							
		                        ###tribal medieval headgears	
				             OR = {
				             EPE_armor_era_tribal_medieval_trigger = yes	##High to late era			
				             current_date <= 1044.1.1	##early era				
				                  }			
								}									
						}
						}
						AND = {
							exists = primary_spouse
							OR = {
								is_ruler = no
								highest_held_title_tier = tier_barony
							}
							primary_spouse = {
								highest_held_title_tier = tier_kingdom
								NOR = {
									AND = {
									has_government = republic_government
										primary_title.tier > 1
									}
									AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
								}
						         OR = {
								  AND = {
					               portrait_northern_clothing_spouse_trigger = yes	
                                   portrait_northern_non_christian_trigger = yes					
								   }
								  AND = {
					               portrait_balto_finnic_clothing_spouse_trigger = yes	
                                   NOT = { portrait_norse_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes } 					
								   }
								 AND = {
				                 portrait_west_slav_clothing_spouse_trigger = yes
                                 NOT = { portrait_norse_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes }							
		                         ###tribal medieval headgears	
				                 OR = {
				                 EPE_armor_era_tribal_medieval_trigger = yes	##High to late era			
				                 current_date <= 1044.1.1	##early era				
				                  }			
								 }								   
						        }
							}
						}
					}
				}
				trigger_else = {
					scope:highest_held_title_tier = tier_kingdom
						OR = {
								AND = {
					             portrait_northern_clothing_trigger = yes	
                                 portrait_northern_non_christian_trigger = yes 					
								}
								AND = {
					             portrait_balto_finnic_clothing_trigger = yes	
                                 NOT = { portrait_norse_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes } 					
								}
								AND = {
				                portrait_west_slav_clothing_trigger = yes
                                NOT = { portrait_norse_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes }							
		                      ###tribal medieval headgears	
				             OR = {
				             EPE_armor_era_tribal_medieval_trigger = yes	##High to late era			
				              current_date <= 1044.1.1	##early era				
				                  }			
								}									
						}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	northern_christian_commoners = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = clothes_2
				template = northern_christian_commoner_clothes
				range = { 0 1 } # For the randomness to work correctly
			}
			morph = {
				mode = modify_multiply
				gene = gene_bs_bust
				value = 0.8 # Setting this lower to reduce the exaggerated effect of large bust size when wearing clothes
				template = bust_clothes
			}

		}	
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				scope:highest_held_title_tier < tier_county
				trigger_if = {
					limit = { exists = this }
					NOR = {
						AND = {
							exists = primary_spouse
							primary_spouse = {
								highest_held_title_tier > tier_county
							}
						}
						AND = {
							has_government = republic_government
							primary_title.tier > 1
						}
						AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
					}
				}
					portrait_northern_clothing_trigger = yes
					portrait_northern_christian_trigger = yes
			}
		}
	}

	northern_christian_low_nobles = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = clothes_2
				template = northern_christian_low_nobility_clothes
				range = { 0 1 } # For the randomness to work correctly
			}
			morph = {
				mode = modify_multiply
				gene = gene_bs_bust
				value = 0.8 # Setting this lower to reduce the exaggerated effect of large bust size when wearing clothes
				template = bust_clothes
			}

		}	
		weight = {
			base = 0

			modifier = {
				add = 50
				trigger_if = {
					limit = { exists = this }
					OR = {
						AND = {
							highest_held_title_tier = tier_county
							NOR = {
								# has_dynasty = no
								AND = {
									has_government = republic_government
									primary_title.tier > 1
								}
								AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
							}
							portrait_northern_clothing_trigger = yes
							portrait_northern_christian_trigger = yes	
						}
						AND = {
							exists = primary_spouse
							OR = {
								is_ruler = no
								highest_held_title_tier = tier_barony
							}
							primary_spouse = {
								highest_held_title_tier = tier_county
								NOR = {
									# has_dynasty = no
									AND = {
										has_government = republic_government
										primary_title.tier > 1
									}
									AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
								}
								portrait_northern_clothing_spouse_trigger = yes
							    portrait_northern_christian_trigger = yes	
							}
						}
					}
				}
				trigger_else = {
					scope:highest_held_title_tier = tier_county
					portrait_northern_clothing_trigger = yes
					portrait_northern_christian_trigger = yes	
				}
			}
		}
	}

	northern_war = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = clothes
				template = northern_war_nobility_clothes
				range = { 0 1 } # For the randomness to work correctly
			}
			morph = {
				mode = modify_multiply
				gene = gene_bs_bust
				value = 0.8 # Setting this lower to reduce the exaggerated effect of large bust size when wearing clothes
				template = bust_clothes
			}

		}
		outfit_tags = { military_outfit }
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 101
				portrait_wear_armor_trigger = yes
						OR = {
							portrait_northern_clothing_trigger = yes
								AND = {
					             portrait_balto_finnic_clothing_trigger = yes	
                                 NOT = { portrait_norse_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes } 					
								}								
						}
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 21		# So priests who are actively serving in a raised army as a commander or knight wear armor instead of priest robes
				portrait_wear_armor_currently_in_army_trigger = yes
			}
		}
	}
	
## FP1 NORSE ##

	fp1_norse_commoners = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = clothes
				template = fp1_commoner_clothes
				range = { 0 1 } # For the randomness to work correctly
			}
			morph = {
				mode = modify_multiply
				gene = gene_bs_bust
				value = 0.8 # Setting this lower to reduce the exaggerated effect of large bust size when wearing clothes
				template = bust_clothes
			}

		}	
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 50 # Slightly higher
				scope:highest_held_title_tier < tier_county
				trigger_if = {
					limit = { exists = this }
					NOR = {
						AND = {
							exists = primary_spouse
							primary_spouse = {
								highest_held_title_tier > tier_county
							}
						}
						has_government = republic_government
						AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
					}
				}
						portrait_fp1_norse_clothing_trigger = yes
						portrait_northern_non_christian_trigger = yes 	
			}
		}
	}

								is_ruler = no

							}
							primary_spouse = {
								highest_held_title_tier = tier_county
								NOR = {
									# has_dynasty = no
									AND = {
										has_government = republic_government
										primary_title.tier > 1
									}
									AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
								}
								portrait_fp1_norse_clothing_spouse_trigger = yes
								portrait_northern_non_christian_trigger = yes 
							}
						}
					}
				}
				trigger_else = {
					scope:highest_held_title_tier = tier_county
					portrait_fp1_norse_clothing_trigger = yes
					portrait_northern_non_christian_trigger = yes 						
				}
			}
		}
	}

	sub_saharan_commoners = {
		dna_modifiers = {

				mode = add

				template = sub_saharan_commoner_clothes

			}
			morph = {

				template = bust_clothes
			}

		}	
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				scope:highest_held_title_tier < tier_county
				trigger_if = {
					limit = { exists = this }
					NOR = {
						AND = {
							exists = primary_spouse
							primary_spouse = {
								highest_held_title_tier > tier_county
							}
						}
						AND = {
							has_government = republic_government
							primary_title.tier > 1
						}
						AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
					}
				}
				portrait_african_clothing_trigger = yes
			}
		}
	}

## Orthodox ##

	religious_orthodox_devoted_clothes = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = clothes
				template = religious_orthodox_devoted_clothes
				range = { 0 1 } # For the randomness to work correctly
			}
			morph = {
				mode = modify_multiply
				gene = gene_bs_bust
				value = 1.0
				template = bust_clothes
			}
			morph = {
				mode = modify_multiply
				gene = gene_bs_body_shape
				value = 1.0
				template = body_shape_average_clothed
			}
		}
		outfit_tags = { monk_outfit }
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 101
				is_male = yes
				OR = {
					has_trait = devoted
					AND = {
						faith = {
							has_doctrine_parameter = vows_of_poverty_active
						}
						OR = {
							AND = {
								has_council_position = councillor_court_chaplain
								faith = {
									has_doctrine = doctrine_theocracy_lay_clergy
								}
							}
							is_theocratic_lessee = yes
							AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
						}
					}
				}
				OR = {
					faith = faith:orthodox
					faith = faith:armenian_apostolic
					faith = faith:coptic
					faith = faith:messalian
					faith = faith:bogomilist
					faith = faith:paulician
					faith = faith:nestorian
				}
			}
		}
	}

				add = 150
				EPE_armor_era_late_medieval_trigger = yes
				exists = this
				faith = faith:catholic
				exists = primary_title
				primary_title = {
					is_head_of_faith = yes
				}
				NOT = {
					faith = {
						has_doctrine_parameter = vows_of_poverty_active
					}
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 150
				EPE_armor_era_late_medieval_trigger = yes
				faith = faith:catholic
				exists = global_var:Trinity_is_loaded
				CatholicTrinityCardinalOrAntiPopeTrigger = yes

	    }

					has_character_flag = need_priest_outfit

##########################################################################
#### Han Chinese
##########################################################################

	han_commoners = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = clothes
				template = han_commoner_clothes
				range = { 0 1 } # For the randomness to work correctly
			}
			morph = {
				mode = modify_multiply
				gene = gene_bs_bust
				value = 1.0 # Setting this lower to reduce the exaggerated effect of large bust size when wearing clothes
				template = bust_clothes
			}

		}	
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 20
				scope:highest_held_title_tier < tier_county
				trigger_if = {
					limit = { exists = this }
					NOR = {
						AND = {
							exists = primary_spouse
							primary_spouse = {
								highest_held_title_tier > tier_county
							}
						}
						AND = {
							has_government = republic_government
							primary_title.tier > 1
						}
						AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
					}
				}
				portrait_han_clothing_trigger = yes
			}
		}
	}

	han_low_nobles = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = clothes
				template = han_low_nobility_clothes
				range = { 0 1 } # For the randomness to work correctly
			}
			morph = {
				mode = modify_multiply
				gene = gene_bs_bust
				value = 1.0 # Setting this lower to reduce the exaggerated effect of large bust size when wearing clothes
				template = bust_clothes
			}

		}	
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				exists = court_owner
				court_owner = {
					has_royal_court = yes
					amenity_level = { type = court_fashion value >= 4 }
				}
				portrait_han_clothing_trigger = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				trigger_if = {
					limit = { exists = this }
					OR = {
						AND = {
							highest_held_title_tier = tier_county
							NOR = {
								# has_dynasty = no
								AND = {
									has_government = republic_government
									primary_title.tier > 1
								}
								AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
							}
							portrait_han_clothing_trigger = yes
						}
						AND = {
							exists = primary_spouse
							OR = {
								is_ruler = no
								highest_held_title_tier = tier_barony
							}
							primary_spouse = {
								highest_held_title_tier = tier_county
								NOR = {
									# has_dynasty = no
									AND = {
										has_government = republic_government
										primary_title.tier > 1
									}
									AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
								}
								portrait_han_clothing_spouse_trigger = yes
							}
						}
					}
				}
				trigger_else = {
					scope:highest_held_title_tier = tier_county
					portrait_han_clothing_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}

	han_high_nobles = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = clothes
				template = han_high_nobility_clothes
				range = { 0 1 } # For the randomness to work correctly
			}
			morph = {
				mode = modify_multiply
				gene = gene_bs_bust
				value = 1.0 # Setting this lower to reduce the exaggerated effect of large bust size when wearing clothes
				template = bust_clothes
			}

		}		
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				exists = court_owner
				court_owner = {
					has_royal_court = yes
					amenity_level = { type = court_fashion value = 5 }
				}
				portrait_han_clothing_trigger = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				trigger_if = {
					limit = { exists = this }
					OR = {
						AND = {
							OR = {
								highest_held_title_tier > tier_county
								AND = {
									has_government = republic_government
									primary_title.tier > 1
								}
								AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
							}
							NOT = { AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = mercenary_government  } } # Blocked for mercenaries
							portrait_han_clothing_trigger = yes
						}
						AND = {
							exists = primary_spouse
							OR = {
								is_ruler = no
								highest_held_title_tier = tier_barony
							}
							primary_spouse = {
								OR = {
									highest_held_title_tier > tier_county
									AND = {
									has_government = republic_government
										primary_title.tier > 1
									}
									AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
								}
								portrait_han_clothing_spouse_trigger = yes
							}
						}
					}	
				}
				trigger_else = {
					scope:highest_held_title_tier > tier_county
					portrait_han_clothing_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}

	han_war = {
		dna_modifiers = {

				template = bust_clothes

		}	
		outfit_tags = { military_outfit }
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 101
				portrait_wear_armor_trigger = yes
				portrait_han_clothing_trigger = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 21		# So priests who are actively serving in a raised army as a commander or knight wear armor instead of priest robes
				portrait_wear_armor_currently_in_army_trigger = yes
			}
		}
	}

					limit = { exists = this }
					NOR = {
						AND = {
							exists = primary_spouse

								highest_held_title_tier > tier_county
							}
						}
						AND = {
							has_government = republic_government
							primary_title.tier > 1
						}
						AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
					}
				}
				portrait_central_african_clothing_trigger = yes

						}
						AND = {
							has_government = republic_government
							primary_title.tier > 1
						}
						AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
					}
				}
				portrait_south_indian_clothing_trigger = yes
			}
		}
	}

	south_indian_low_nobles = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = clothes
				template = south_indian_low_nobility_clothes
				range = { 0 1 } # For the randomness to work correctly
			}
			morph = {
				mode = modify_multiply
				gene = gene_bs_bust
				value = 1.0 # Setting this lower to reduce the exaggerated effect of large bust size when wearing clothes
				template = bust_clothes_light
			}

		}	
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				exists = court_owner
				court_owner = {
					has_royal_court = yes
					amenity_level = { type = court_fashion value >= 4 }
				}
				portrait_south_indian_clothing_trigger = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				trigger_if = {
					limit = { exists = this }
					OR = {
						AND = {
							highest_held_title_tier = tier_county
							NOR = {
								# has_dynasty = no
								AND = {
									has_government = republic_government
									primary_title.tier > 1
								}
								AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
							}
							portrait_south_indian_clothing_trigger = yes
						}
						AND = {
							exists = primary_spouse
							OR = {
								is_ruler = no
								highest_held_title_tier = tier_barony
							}
							primary_spouse = {
								highest_held_title_tier = tier_county
								NOR = {
									# has_dynasty = no
									AND = {
										has_government = republic_government
										primary_title.tier > 1
									}
									AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
								}
								portrait_south_indian_clothing_spouse_trigger = yes
							}
						}
					}
				}
				trigger_else = {
					scope:highest_held_title_tier = tier_county
					portrait_south_indian_clothing_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}

	south_indian_high_nobles = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = clothes
				template = south_indian_high_nobility_clothes
				range = { 0 1 } # For the randomness to work correctly
			}
			morph = {
				mode = modify_multiply
				gene = gene_bs_bust
				value = 1.0 # Setting this lower to reduce the exaggerated effect of large bust size when wearing clothes
				template = bust_clothes_light
			}

		}		
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				exists = court_owner
				court_owner = {
					has_royal_court = yes
					amenity_level = { type = court_fashion value = 5 }
				}
				portrait_south_indian_clothing_trigger = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				trigger_if = {
					limit = { exists = this }
					OR = {
						AND = {
							OR = {
								highest_held_title_tier > tier_county
								AND = {
									has_government = republic_government
									primary_title.tier > 1
								}
								AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
							}
							NOT = { AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = mercenary_government  } } # Blocked for mercenaries
							portrait_south_indian_clothing_trigger = yes
						}
						AND = {
							exists = primary_spouse
							OR = {
								is_ruler = no
								highest_held_title_tier = tier_barony
							}
							primary_spouse = {
								OR = {
									highest_held_title_tier > tier_county
									AND = {
									has_government = republic_government
										primary_title.tier > 1
									}
									AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
								}
								portrait_south_indian_clothing_spouse_trigger = yes
							}
						}
					}	
				}
				trigger_else = {
					scope:highest_held_title_tier > tier_county
					portrait_south_indian_clothing_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}

	south_indian_war = {

		}

			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 101
				portrait_wear_armor_trigger = yes
				portrait_south_indian_clothing_trigger = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 21		# So priests who are actively serving in a raised army as a commander or knight wear armor instead of priest robes
				portrait_wear_armor_currently_in_army_trigger = yes
			}
		}
	}

	french_commoners = {

				value = 0.8 # Setting this lower to reduce the exaggerated effect of large bust size when wearing clothes
				template = bust_clothes
			}

		}	
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 20
				scope:highest_held_title_tier < tier_county
				trigger_if = {
					limit = { exists = this }
					NOR = {
						AND = {
							exists = primary_spouse
							primary_spouse = {
								highest_held_title_tier > tier_county
							}
						}
						AND = {
							has_government = republic_government
							primary_title.tier > 1
						}
						AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
					}
				}
				portrait_french_clothing_trigger = yes
				portrait_dde_hre_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes	
				#portrait_western_christian_trigger = yes
			}
		}
	}

				             portrait_dde_hre_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes	
							 #portrait_western_christian_trigger = yes
							}
						}
					}
				}
				trigger_else = {
					scope:highest_held_title_tier = tier_county
				 portrait_italian_clothing_trigger = yes
				 portrait_dde_hre_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes	
				 #portrait_western_christian_trigger = yes
				}

		}

	italian_high_nobles = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = clothes
				template = italian_high_nobility_clothes
				range = { 0 1 } # For the randomness to work correctly
			}
			morph = {
				mode = modify_multiply
				gene = gene_bs_bust
				value = 0.8 # Setting this lower to reduce the exaggerated effect of large bust size when wearing clothes
				template = bust_clothes
			}

		}		
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				exists = court_owner
				court_owner = {
					has_royal_court = yes
					amenity_level = { type = court_fashion value = 5 }
				}
				portrait_italian_clothing_trigger = yes
				portrait_dde_hre_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes	
				#portrait_western_christian_trigger = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				trigger_if = {
					limit = { exists = this }
					OR = {
						AND = {
							OR = {
								highest_held_title_tier > tier_county
								AND = {
									has_government = republic_government
									primary_title.tier > 1
								}
								AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
							}
							NOT = { AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = mercenary_government  } } # Blocked for mercenaries
				            portrait_italian_clothing_trigger = yes
				            portrait_dde_hre_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes
							#portrait_western_christian_trigger = yes
						}
						AND = {
							exists = primary_spouse
							OR = {
								is_ruler = no
								highest_held_title_tier = tier_barony
							}
							primary_spouse = {
								OR = {
									highest_held_title_tier > tier_county
									AND = {
									has_government = republic_government
										primary_title.tier > 1
									}
									AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
								}
							 portrait_italian_clothing_spouse_trigger = yes
				             portrait_dde_hre_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes	
							 #portrait_western_christian_trigger = yes
							}
						}
					}	
				}
				trigger_else = {
					scope:highest_held_title_tier > tier_county
				 portrait_italian_clothing_trigger = yes
				 portrait_dde_hre_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes
				 #portrait_western_christian_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}

	italian_war = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = clothes
				template = italian_war_nobility_clothes
				range = { 0 1 } # For the randomness to work correctly
			}
			morph = {
				mode = modify_multiply

				template = bust_clothes
			}

		}	
		outfit_tags = { military_outfit }
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 101
				portrait_wear_armor_trigger = yes
				portrait_italian_clothing_trigger = yes
				portrait_dde_hre_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes	
			 OR = {
				EPE_armor_era_late_medieval_trigger = yes	
				EPE_armor_era_high_medieval_trigger = yes						
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 21		# So priests who are actively serving in a raised army as a commander or knight wear armor instead of priest robes
				portrait_wear_armor_currently_in_army_trigger = yes
			}
		}
	}

				template = bust_clothes
			}

		}	
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 20
				scope:highest_held_title_tier < tier_county
				trigger_if = {
					limit = { exists = this }
					NOR = {
						AND = {
							exists = primary_spouse
							primary_spouse = {
								highest_held_title_tier > tier_county
							}
						}
						AND = {
							has_government = republic_government
							primary_title.tier > 1
						}
						AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
					}
				}
				portrait_italian_clothing_trigger = yes

								}
								AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
							}
							NOT = { AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = mercenary_government  } } # Blocked for mercenaries
				            portrait_dde_abbasid_clothing_trigger = yes
				            EPE_culture_era_high_medieval_trigger = yes	
				            portrait_dde_abbasid_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes	
						}
						AND = {
							exists = primary_spouse
							OR = {
								is_ruler = no
								highest_held_title_tier = tier_barony
							}
							primary_spouse = {
								OR = {
									highest_held_title_tier > tier_county
									AND = {
										has_government = republic_government
										primary_title.tier > 1
									}
									AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
								}
				               portrait_dde_abbasid_clothing_spouse_trigger = yes
				               EPE_culture_era_high_medieval_trigger = yes	
				               portrait_dde_abbasid_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes	
							}
						}
					}
				}
				trigger_else = {
					scope:highest_held_title_tier > tier_county
				 portrait_dde_abbasid_clothing_trigger = yes
				 EPE_culture_era_high_medieval_trigger = yes	
				 portrait_dde_abbasid_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes	
				}
			}
		}
	}

	dde_high_abbasid_war = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = clothes
				template = dde_high_abbasid_war_nobility_clothes
				range = { 0 1 } # For the randomness to work correctly
			}
			morph = {
				mode = modify_multiply
				gene = gene_bs_bust
				value = 0.8 # Setting this lower to reduce the exaggerated effect of large bust size when wearing clothes
				template = bust_clothes
			}

		}
		outfit_tags = { military_outfit }
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 101
				portrait_wear_armor_trigger = yes
				portrait_dde_abbasid_clothing_trigger = yes
				EPE_culture_era_high_medieval_trigger = yes	
				portrait_dde_abbasid_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes	
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 21		# So priests who are actively serving in a raised army as a commander or knight wear armor instead of priest robes
				portrait_wear_armor_currently_in_army_trigger = yes
			}
		}
	}

				template = bust_clothes
			}

		}	
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 100
				scope:highest_held_title_tier < tier_county
				trigger_if = {
					limit = { exists = this }
					NOR = {
						AND = {
							exists = primary_spouse
							primary_spouse = {
								highest_held_title_tier > tier_county
							}
						}
						has_government = republic_government
						AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
					}
				}
				portrait_tahirid_trigger = yes
				EPE_culture_era_early_medieval_trigger = yes	
			}
		}

				template = bust_clothes
			}

		}	
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				exists = court_owner
				court_owner = {
					has_royal_court = yes
					amenity_level = { type = court_fashion value >= 4 }
				}
				portrait_east_african_clothing_trigger = yes
				portrait_east_african_non_islamic_trigger = yes	
				NOT = { portrait_nubian_clothing_trigger = yes } 
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				trigger_if = {
					limit = { exists = this }
					OR = {
						AND = {
							highest_held_title_tier = tier_county
							NOR = {
								# has_dynasty = no
								AND = {
									has_government = republic_government
									primary_title.tier > 1
								}
								AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
							}
							portrait_east_african_clothing_trigger = yes
							portrait_east_african_non_islamic_trigger = yes	
				            NOT = { portrait_nubian_clothing_trigger = yes } 	
						}
						AND = {
							exists = primary_spouse
							OR = {
								is_ruler = no
								highest_held_title_tier = tier_barony
							}
							primary_spouse = {
								highest_held_title_tier = tier_county
								NOR = {
									# has_dynasty = no
									AND = {
										has_government = republic_government
										primary_title.tier > 1
									}
									AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
								}
								portrait_east_african_clothing_spouse_trigger = yes
							    portrait_east_african_non_islamic_trigger = yes	
				                NOT = { portrait_nubian_clothing_spouse_trigger = yes } 	
							}
						}
					}
					
				}
				trigger_else = {
					scope:highest_held_title_tier = tier_county
					portrait_east_african_clothing_trigger = yes
					portrait_east_african_non_islamic_trigger = yes	
				    NOT = { portrait_nubian_clothing_trigger = yes }
				}

				value = 0.8 # Setting this lower to reduce the exaggerated effect of large bust size when wearing clothes
				template = bust_clothes
			}

		}		
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				exists = court_owner
				court_owner = {
					has_royal_court = yes
					amenity_level = { type = court_fashion value = 5 }
				}
				portrait_early_eastern_iranian_trigger = yes
				portrait_non_iranian_trigger = yes	
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				trigger_if = {
					limit = { exists = this }
					OR = {
						AND = {
							OR = {
								highest_held_title_tier > tier_county
								AND = {
									has_government = republic_government
									primary_title.tier > 1
								}
								AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
							}
							NOT = { AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = mercenary_government  } } # Blocked for mercenaries
				            portrait_early_eastern_iranian_trigger = yes
				            portrait_non_iranian_trigger = yes	
						}
						AND = {
							exists = primary_spouse
							OR = {
								is_ruler = no
								highest_held_title_tier = tier_barony
							}
							primary_spouse = {
								OR = {
									highest_held_title_tier > tier_county
									AND = {
										has_government = republic_government
										primary_title.tier > 1
									}
									AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
								}
					            portrait_early_eastern_iranian_spouse_trigger = yes	
						        portrait_non_iranian_trigger = yes		
							}
						}
					}
				}
				trigger_else = {
					scope:highest_held_title_tier > tier_county
				    portrait_early_eastern_iranian_trigger = yes
				    portrait_non_iranian_trigger = yes	
				}
			}
		}
	}

	early_eastern_iranian_war = {

		}

				template = bust_clothes
			}

		}	
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 40
				scope:highest_held_title_tier < tier_county
				trigger_if = {
					limit = { exists = this }
					NOR = {
						AND = {
							exists = primary_spouse
							primary_spouse = {
								highest_held_title_tier > tier_county
							}
						}
						has_government = republic_government
						AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
					}
				}
				portrait_iranian_clothing_trigger = yes	
				portrait_islamic_iranian_trigger = yes	
				EPE_culture_era_early_medieval_trigger = yes
			}
		}
	}

							highest_held_title_tier = tier_county
							NOR = {
								# has_dynasty = no
								AND = {
									has_government = republic_government
									primary_title.tier > 1
								}
								AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
							}
				            portrait_northern_slav_clothing_trigger = yes
				            NOT = { portrait_norse_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes }		
						}
						AND = {
							exists = primary_spouse
							OR = {
								is_ruler = no
								highest_held_title_tier = tier_barony
							}
							primary_spouse = {
								highest_held_title_tier = tier_county
								NOR = {
									# has_dynasty = no
									AND = {
										has_government = republic_government
										primary_title.tier > 1
									}
									AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
								}
							    portrait_northern_slav_clothing_spouse_trigger = yes
							    NOT = { portrait_norse_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes }		
							}
						}
					}
					
				}
				trigger_else = {
					scope:highest_held_title_tier = tier_county
				    portrait_northern_slav_clothing_trigger = yes
				    NOT = { portrait_norse_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes }		
				}
			}
		}
	}

	northern_slav_non_dlc_high_nobles = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = clothes
				template = northern_high_nobility_clothes
				range = { 0 1 } # For the randomness to work correctly
			}
			morph = {
				mode = modify_multiply
				gene = gene_bs_bust
				value = 0.8 # Setting this lower to reduce the exaggerated effect of large bust size when wearing clothes
				template = bust_clothes
			}

		}	
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				exists = court_owner
				court_owner = {
					has_royal_court = yes
					amenity_level = { type = court_fashion value = 5 }
				}
				portrait_northern_slav_clothing_trigger = yes
				NOT = { portrait_norse_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes }		
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				trigger_if = {
					limit = { exists = this }
					OR = {
						AND = {
							OR = {
								highest_held_title_tier = tier_duchy
								AND = {
									has_government = republic_government
									primary_title.tier > 1
								}
								AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
							}
							NOT = { AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = mercenary_government  } } # Blocked for mercenaries
				            portrait_northern_slav_clothing_trigger = yes
				            NOT = { portrait_norse_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes }		
						}
						AND = {
							exists = primary_spouse
							OR = {
								is_ruler = no
								highest_held_title_tier = tier_barony
							}
							primary_spouse = {
								OR = {
									highest_held_title_tier = tier_duchy
									AND = {
										has_government = republic_government
										primary_title.tier > 1
									}
									AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
								}
							    portrait_northern_slav_clothing_spouse_trigger = yes
							    NOT = { portrait_norse_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes }										
							}
						}
					}
				}
				trigger_else = {
					scope:highest_held_title_tier = tier_duchy
				    portrait_northern_slav_clothing_trigger = yes
				    NOT = { portrait_norse_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes }		
				}
			}
		}
	}

##########################################################################
#### Cuman early medieval
##########################################################################

	cuman_early_commoners = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = clothes
				template = cuman_commoner_clothes
				range = { 0 1 } # For the randomness to work correctly
			}
			morph = {
				mode = modify_multiply
				gene = gene_bs_bust
				value = 1.0
				template = bust_clothes
			}

		}	
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 20
				scope:highest_held_title_tier < tier_county
				trigger_if = {
					limit = { exists = this }
					NOR = {
						AND = {
							exists = primary_spouse
							primary_spouse = {
								highest_held_title_tier > tier_county
							}
						}
						AND = {
							has_government = republic_government
							primary_title.tier > 1
						}
						AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
					}
				}
				portrait_cuman_clothing_trigger = yes
			}
		}
	}

	cuman_early_low_nobles = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = clothes
				template = cuman_low_nobility_clothes

			}
			morph = {
				mode = modify_multiply

		}

				court_owner = {
					has_royal_court = yes
					amenity_level = { type = court_fashion value >= 4 }
				}
				portrait_cuman_clothing_trigger = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 50

							highest_held_title_tier = tier_county
							NOR = {
								# has_dynasty = no

									has_government = republic_government
									primary_title.tier > 1
								}
								AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
							}

						}

							exists = primary_spouse

								is_ruler = no
								highest_held_title_tier = tier_barony
							}

								highest_held_title_tier = tier_county
								NOR = {
									# has_dynasty = no
									AND = {
									has_government = republic_government
										primary_title.tier > 1
									}
									AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
								}
								portrait_cuman_clothing_spouse_trigger = yes
							}
						}
					}
				}

				}

		}

				mode = add

				template = cuman_war_nobility_clothes
				range = { 0 1 } # For the randomness to work correctly
			}
			morph = {
				mode = modify_multiply
				gene = gene_bs_bust
				value = 1.0
				template = bust_clothes
			}

		}
		outfit_tags = { military_outfit }
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 101
				portrait_wear_armor_trigger = yes
				portrait_cuman_clothing_trigger = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 21		# So priests who are actively serving in a raised army as a commander or knight wear armor instead of priest robes
				portrait_wear_armor_currently_in_army_trigger = yes
			}
		}
	}

	balto_finnic_commoners = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = clothes
				template = balto_finnic_commoner_clothes
				range = { 0 1 } # For the randomness to work correctly
			}
			morph = {
				mode = modify_multiply
				gene = gene_bs_bust
				value = 1.0
				template = bust_clothes
			}

		}	
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 20
				scope:highest_held_title_tier < tier_county
				trigger_if = {
					limit = { exists = this }
					NOR = {
						AND = {
							exists = primary_spouse
							primary_spouse = {
								highest_held_title_tier > tier_county
							}
						}
						AND = {
							has_government = republic_government
							primary_title.tier > 1
						}
						AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
					}
				}
				portrait_balto_finnic_clothing_trigger = yes					
				portrait_norse_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes					
			}
		}
	}

	balto_finnic_low_nobles = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = clothes
				template = balto_finnic_low_nobility_clothes
				range = { 0 1 } # For the randomness to work correctly
			}
			morph = {
				mode = modify_multiply
				gene = gene_bs_bust
				value = 1.0
				template = bust_clothes
			}

		}	
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				exists = court_owner
				court_owner = {
					has_royal_court = yes
					amenity_level = { type = court_fashion value >= 4 }
				}
				portrait_balto_finnic_clothing_trigger = yes					
				portrait_norse_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes					
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				trigger_if = {
					limit = { exists = this }
					OR = {
						AND = {
							highest_held_title_tier = tier_county
							NOR = {
								# has_dynasty = no
								AND = {
									has_government = republic_government
									primary_title.tier > 1
								}
								AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
							}
				            portrait_balto_finnic_clothing_trigger = yes					
				            portrait_norse_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes								
						}
						AND = {
							exists = primary_spouse
							OR = {
								is_ruler = no
								highest_held_title_tier = tier_barony
							}
							primary_spouse = {
								highest_held_title_tier = tier_county
								NOR = {
									# has_dynasty = no
									AND = {
									has_government = republic_government
										primary_title.tier > 1
									}
									AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
								}
							    portrait_balto_finnic_clothing_spouse_trigger = yes					
							    portrait_norse_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes		
							}
						}
					}
				}
				trigger_else = {
					scope:highest_held_title_tier = tier_county
				    portrait_balto_finnic_clothing_trigger = yes					
				    portrait_norse_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes						
				}
			}
		}
	}

							OR = {
								is_ruler = no
								highest_held_title_tier = tier_barony
							}
							primary_spouse = {
								highest_held_title_tier = tier_county
								NOR = {
									# has_dynasty = no
									AND = {
									 has_government = republic_government
										primary_title.tier > 1
									}
									AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
								}
							     portrait_polish_clothing_spouse_trigger = yes
								 #portrait_western_christian_trigger = yes
		                             ###High medieval headgears		
				                  OR = {
				                 EPE_culture_era_high_medieval_trigger = yes	##High to late era			
				                 current_date >= 1026.1.1	##early era				
				                    }				
							    		
							}
						}
					}
				}
				trigger_else = {
					scope:highest_held_title_tier = tier_county
				    portrait_polish_clothing_trigger = yes
					#portrait_western_christian_trigger = yes
		            ###High medieval headgears		
				    OR = {
				    EPE_culture_era_high_medieval_trigger = yes	##High to late era			
				    current_date >= 1026.1.1	##early era				
				       }					
				    						
				}
			}
		}

	polish_high_nobles = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = clothes
				template = polish_high_nobility_clothes
				range = { 0 1 } # For the randomness to work correctly
			}
			morph = {
				mode = modify_multiply
				gene = gene_bs_bust
				value = 0.8 # Setting this lower to reduce the exaggerated effect of large bust size when wearing clothes
				template = bust_clothes
			}

		}		
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				exists = court_owner
				court_owner = {
					has_royal_court = yes
					amenity_level = { type = court_fashion value = 5 }
				}
				portrait_polish_clothing_trigger = yes
				#portrait_western_christian_trigger = yes
		###High medieval headgears		
				OR = {
				EPE_culture_era_high_medieval_trigger = yes	##High to late era			
				current_date >= 1026.1.1	##early era				
				}				
									
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				trigger_if = {
					limit = { exists = this }
					OR = {
						AND = {
							OR = {
								highest_held_title_tier > tier_county
								AND = {
									has_government = republic_government
									primary_title.tier > 1
								}
								AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
							}
							NOT = { AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = mercenary_government  } } # Blocked for mercenaries
				             portrait_polish_clothing_trigger = yes
							 #portrait_western_christian_trigger = yes
		                     ###High medieval headgears		
				             OR = {
				             EPE_culture_era_high_medieval_trigger = yes	##High to late era			
				             current_date >= 1026.1.1	##early era				
				                }						
				            								
						}
						AND = {
							exists = primary_spouse
							OR = {
								is_ruler = no
								highest_held_title_tier = tier_barony
							}
							primary_spouse = {
								OR = {
									highest_held_title_tier > tier_county
									AND = {
										has_government = republic_government
										primary_title.tier > 1
									}
									AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
								}
							     portrait_polish_clothing_spouse_trigger = yes
								 #portrait_western_christian_trigger = yes
		                             ###High medieval headgears		
				                  OR = {
				                 EPE_culture_era_high_medieval_trigger = yes	##High to late era			
				                 current_date >= 1026.1.1	##early era				
				                    }				
							    										
							}
						}
					}
				}
				trigger_else = {
					scope:highest_held_title_tier > tier_county
				    portrait_polish_clothing_trigger = yes
					#portrait_western_christian_trigger = yes
		            ###High medieval headgears		
				    OR = {
				    EPE_culture_era_high_medieval_trigger = yes	##High to late era			
				    current_date >= 1026.1.1	##early era				
				       }				    						
				}
			}
		}
	}

	polish_war = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = clothes
				template = polish_war_nobility_clothes
				range = { 0 1 } # For the randomness to work correctly
			}
			morph = {
				mode = modify_multiply
				gene = gene_bs_bust
				value = 0.8 # Setting this lower to reduce the exaggerated effect of large bust size when wearing clothes
				template = bust_clothes
			}

		}
		outfit_tags = { military_outfit }
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 101
				portrait_wear_armor_trigger = yes
				portrait_polish_clothing_trigger = yes
			 OR = {
				EPE_armor_era_late_medieval_trigger = yes	
				EPE_armor_era_high_medieval_trigger = yes						
				}										
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 21		# So priests who are actively serving in a raised army as a commander or knight wear armor instead of priest robes
				portrait_wear_armor_currently_in_army_trigger = yes
			}
		}
	}

		                     ###tribal medieval headgears

				                  }

							}

									# has_dynasty = no

									has_government = republic_government
										primary_title.tier > 1
									}

								}

		                             ###tribal medieval headgears

				                EPE_armor_era_tribal_medieval_trigger = yes	##High to late era			
				                current_date <= 1044.1.1	##early era				
				                      }				
							    		
							}
						}
					}
				}
				trigger_else = {
					scope:highest_held_title_tier = tier_county
				    portrait_west_slav_clothing_trigger = yes
				    portrait_norse_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes						
		            ###tribal medieval headgears

####Tribal Era Crusader
	tribal_era_teutonic_crusades = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = clothes
				template = tribal_era_western_war
				range = { 0 1 } # For the randomness to work correctly
			}
			morph = {
				mode = modify_multiply
				gene = gene_bs_bust
				value = 0.8 # Setting this lower to reduce the exaggerated effect of large bust size when wearing clothes
				template = bust_clothes
			}

		}
		outfit_tags = { military_outfit }
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 110
				exists = this
				EPE_armor_era_tribal_medieval_trigger = yes	
				OR = {
					portrait_german_clothing_spouse_trigger = yes
				    portrait_dde_hre_clothing_spouse_trigger = yes						
				}					
				OR = {
				    AND = {
				        religion = religion:christianity_religion
				        has_government = holy_order_government
				    }
				    AND = {
				        religion = religion:christianity_religion
				        is_landed = no
				        portrait_wear_armor_trigger = yes
				        exists = liege
				        liege = {
				            has_government = holy_order_government
				        }
				    }
				    AND = {
				        religion = religion:christianity_religion
				        portrait_wear_armor_trigger = yes
				        OR = {
				            any_character_war = {
				                OR = {
				                    using_cb = undirected_great_holy_war
				                    using_cb = directed_great_holy_war
				                }
				                is_attacker = prev
				            }
				            any_liege_or_above = {
				                faith = prev.faith
				                any_character_war = {
				                    OR = {
				                        using_cb = undirected_great_holy_war
				                        using_cb = directed_great_holy_war
				                    }
				                    is_attacker = prev
				                }
				            }
				        }
				    }
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 21		# So priests who are actively serving in a raised army as a commander or knight wear armor instead of priest robes
				EPE_armor_era_tribal_medieval_trigger = yes						
				portrait_wear_armor_currently_in_army_trigger = yes
			}
		}
	}
	
####Early Era teutonic Crusader
	
	early_era_teutonic_crusades = {

			}

				mode = modify_multiply

				value = 0.8 # Setting this lower to reduce the exaggerated effect of large bust size when wearing clothes
				template = bust_clothes
			}

		}
		outfit_tags = { military_outfit }

			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 110

				OR = {
					portrait_german_clothing_spouse_trigger = yes
				    portrait_dde_hre_clothing_spouse_trigger = yes						
				}

				    AND = {
				        religion = religion:christianity_religion
				        has_government = holy_order_government
				    }
				    AND = {
				        religion = religion:christianity_religion
				        is_landed = no
				        portrait_wear_armor_trigger = yes
				        exists = liege
				        liege = {
				            has_government = holy_order_government
				        }
				    }
				    AND = {
				        religion = religion:christianity_religion
				        portrait_wear_armor_trigger = yes
				        OR = {
				            any_character_war = {
				                OR = {
				                    using_cb = undirected_great_holy_war
				                    using_cb = directed_great_holy_war
				                }
				                is_attacker = prev
				            }

				                    OR = {
				                        using_cb = undirected_great_holy_war
				                        using_cb = directed_great_holy_war
				                    }
				                    is_attacker = prev
				                }
				            }
				        }
				    }
				}
			}

			}
