#### German
##########################################################################

	german_commoners = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = clothes
				template = german_commoner_clothes
				range = { 0 1 } # For the randomness to work correctly
			}
			morph = {
				mode = modify_multiply
				gene = gene_bs_bust
				value = 0.8 # Setting this lower to reduce the exaggerated effect of large bust size when wearing clothes
				template = bust_clothes
			}

		}	
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 20
				scope:highest_held_title_tier < tier_county
				trigger_if = {
					limit = { exists = this }
					NOR = {
						AND = {
							exists = primary_spouse
							primary_spouse = {
								highest_held_title_tier > tier_county
							}
						}
						AND = {
							has_government = republic_government
							primary_title.tier > 1
						}
						AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
					}
				}
				portrait_german_clothing_trigger = yes
				#portrait_western_christian_trigger = yes
			}
		}
	}

	german_low_nobles = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = clothes
				template = german_low_nobility_clothes
				range = { 0 1 } # For the randomness to work correctly
			}
			morph = {
				mode = modify_multiply
				gene = gene_bs_bust
				value = 0.8 # Setting this lower to reduce the exaggerated effect of large bust size when wearing clothes
				template = bust_clothes
			}

		}	
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				exists = court_owner
				court_owner = {
					has_royal_court = yes
					amenity_level = { type = court_fashion value >= 4 }
				}
				portrait_german_clothing_trigger = yes
				#portrait_western_christian_trigger = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				trigger_if = {
					limit = { exists = this }
					OR = {
						AND = {
							highest_held_title_tier = tier_county
							NOR = {
								# has_dynasty = no
								AND = {
									has_government = republic_government
									primary_title.tier > 1
								}
								AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
							}
							portrait_german_clothing_trigger = yes
							#portrait_western_christian_trigger = yes
						}
						AND = {
							exists = primary_spouse
							OR = {
								is_ruler = no
								highest_held_title_tier = tier_barony
							}
							primary_spouse = {
								highest_held_title_tier = tier_county
								NOR = {
									# has_dynasty = no
									AND = {
									has_government = republic_government
										primary_title.tier > 1
									}
									AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
								}
								portrait_german_clothing_spouse_trigger = yes
								#portrait_western_christian_trigger = yes
							}
						}
					}
				}
				trigger_else = {
					scope:highest_held_title_tier = tier_county
					portrait_german_clothing_trigger = yes
					#portrait_western_christian_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}

			}
			morph = {

				gene = gene_bs_bust

				template = bust_clothes
			}

		}		
		weight = {

				add = 50
				exists = court_owner

				}
				portrait_german_clothing_trigger = yes

					OR = {

								AND = {
									has_government = republic_government
									primary_title.tier > 1
								}
								AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
							}
							NOT = { AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = mercenary_government  } } # Blocked for mercenaries
							portrait_german_clothing_trigger = yes
							#portrait_western_christian_trigger = yes
						}
						AND = {
							exists = primary_spouse
							OR = {
								is_ruler = no
								highest_held_title_tier = tier_barony
							}
							primary_spouse = {
								OR = {
									highest_held_title_tier > tier_county
									AND = {
										has_government = republic_government
										primary_title.tier > 1
									}
									AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
								}
								portrait_german_clothing_spouse_trigger = yes
								#portrait_western_christian_trigger = yes
							}
						}
					}
				}
				trigger_else = {
					scope:highest_held_title_tier > tier_county
					portrait_german_clothing_trigger = yes
					#portrait_western_christian_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}

		}
		outfit_tags = { military_outfit }
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 101
				portrait_wear_armor_trigger = yes
				portrait_burmese_clothing_trigger = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 21		# So priests who are actively serving in a raised army as a commander or knight wear armor instead of priest robes
				portrait_wear_armor_currently_in_army_trigger = yes
			}
		}
	}
	
##########################################################################
#### Sahelian
##########################################################################

	sahelian_commoners = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = clothes
				template = sahelian_commoner_clothes
				range = { 0 0.53 } # For the randomness to work correctly
			}
			morph = {
				mode = modify_multiply
				gene = gene_bs_bust
				value = 0.8 # Setting this lower to reduce the exaggerated effect of large bust size when wearing clothes
				template = bust_clothes
			}

		}	
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 20
				scope:highest_held_title_tier < tier_county
				trigger_if = {
					limit = { exists = this }
					NOR = {
						AND = {
							exists = primary_spouse
							primary_spouse = {
								highest_held_title_tier > tier_county
							}
						}
						AND = {
							has_government = republic_government
							primary_title.tier > 1
						}
						AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
					}
				}
				portrait_sahelian_clothing_trigger = yes
			}
		}
	}

	sahelian_low_nobles = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = clothes
				template = sahelian_low_nobility_clothes
				range = { 0 0.53 } # For the randomness to work correctly
			}
			morph = {
				mode = modify_multiply
				gene = gene_bs_bust
				value = 0.8 # Setting this lower to reduce the exaggerated effect of large bust size when wearing clothes
				template = bust_clothes
			}

		}	
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				exists = court_owner
				court_owner = {
					has_royal_court = yes
					amenity_level = { type = court_fashion value >= 4 }
				}
				portrait_sahelian_clothing_trigger = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				trigger_if = {
					limit = { exists = this }
					OR = {
						AND = {
							highest_held_title_tier = tier_county
							NOR = {
								# has_dynasty = no
								AND = {
									has_government = republic_government
									primary_title.tier > 1
								}
								AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
							}
							portrait_sahelian_clothing_trigger = yes
						}
						AND = {
							exists = primary_spouse
							OR = {
								is_ruler = no
								highest_held_title_tier = tier_barony
							}
							primary_spouse = {
								highest_held_title_tier = tier_county
								NOR = {
									# has_dynasty = no
									AND = {
									has_government = republic_government
										primary_title.tier > 1
									}
									AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
								}
								portrait_sahelian_clothing_spouse_trigger = yes
							}
						}
					}
				}
				trigger_else = {
					scope:highest_held_title_tier = tier_county
					portrait_sahelian_clothing_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}

							portrait_sahelian_clothing_trigger = yes

				template = bulgarian_war_nobility_clothes
				range = { 0 1 } # For the randomness to work correctly
			}
			morph = {
				mode = modify_multiply
				gene = gene_bs_bust
				value = 0.8 # Setting this lower to reduce the exaggerated effect of large bust size when wearing clothes
				template = bust_clothes
			}

		}
		outfit_tags = { military_outfit }
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 101
				portrait_wear_armor_trigger = yes
				portrait_bulgarian_clothing_trigger = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 21		# So priests who are actively serving in a raised army as a commander or knight wear armor instead of priest robes
				portrait_wear_armor_currently_in_army_trigger = yes
			}
		}
	}

			modifier = {
				add = 20
				scope:highest_held_title_tier < tier_county
				trigger_if = {
					limit = { exists = this }
					NOR = {
						AND = {
							exists = primary_spouse
							primary_spouse = {
								highest_held_title_tier > tier_county
							}
						}
						AND = {
							has_government = republic_government
							primary_title.tier > 1
						}
						AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
					}
				}
				portrait_armenian_clothing_trigger = yes
			}

				add = 150

				portrait_northern_clothing_trigger = yes
				OR = {
					has_character_flag = need_priest_outfit
					is_theocratic_lessee = yes
					AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
				}
				religion = religion:christianity_religion
				NAND = {
					faith = faith:catholic
					faith.religious_head = this
				}
				NOT = {
					faith = faith:orthodox
				}
				# NOT = {
					# faith = {
						# has_doctrine_parameter = vows_of_poverty_active

					portrait_andalusian_clothing_trigger = yes	
				    #portrait_east_african_clothing_trigger = yes									
				    #portrait_cuman_clothing_trigger = yes	
				    #portrait_uyghur_clothing_trigger = yes									
					#portrait_indian_clothing_trigger = yes
					#portrait_steppe_clothing_trigger = yes
					#portrait_african_clothing_trigger = yes
				    #portrait_south_indian_clothing_trigger = yes									
			        #portrait_Khazar_clothing_trigger = yes	
			        #portrait_kirghiz_clothing_trigger = yes	
				    #portrait_burmese_clothing_trigger = yes							
				}
			}
		}

##########################################################################
#### Bedouin(arabs)
##########################################################################

	bedouin_commoners = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = clothes
				template = bedouin_commoner_clothes
				range = { 0 1 } # For the randomness to work correctly
			}
			morph = {
				mode = modify_multiply
				gene = gene_bs_bust
				value = 0.8 # Setting this lower to reduce the exaggerated effect of large bust size when wearing clothes
				template = bust_clothes
			}

		}	
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 20
				scope:highest_held_title_tier < tier_county
				trigger_if = {
					limit = { exists = this }
					NOR = {
						AND = {
							exists = primary_spouse
							primary_spouse = {
								highest_held_title_tier > tier_county
							}
						}
						AND = {
							has_government = republic_government
							primary_title.tier > 1
						}
						AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
					}
				}
				portrait_bedouin_clothing_trigger = yes
			}
		}
	}

	bedouin_low_nobles = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = clothes
				template = bedouin_low_nobility_clothes
				range = { 0 1 } # For the randomness to work correctly
			}
			morph = {
				mode = modify_multiply
				gene = gene_bs_bust
				value = 0.8 # Setting this lower to reduce the exaggerated effect of large bust size when wearing clothes
				template = bust_clothes
			}

		}	
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				exists = court_owner
				court_owner = {
					has_royal_court = yes
					amenity_level = { type = court_fashion value >= 4 }
				}
				portrait_bedouin_clothing_trigger = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				trigger_if = {
					limit = { exists = this }
					OR = {
						AND = {
							highest_held_title_tier = tier_county
							NOR = {
								# has_dynasty = no
								AND = {
									has_government = republic_government
									primary_title.tier > 1
								}
								AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
							}
							portrait_bedouin_clothing_trigger = yes
						}
						AND = {
							exists = primary_spouse
							OR = {
								is_ruler = no
								highest_held_title_tier = tier_barony
							}
							primary_spouse = {
								highest_held_title_tier = tier_county
								NOR = {
									# has_dynasty = no
									AND = {
									has_government = republic_government
										primary_title.tier > 1
									}
									AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
								}
								portrait_bedouin_clothing_spouse_trigger = yes
							}
						}
					}
				}
				trigger_else = {
					scope:highest_held_title_tier = tier_county
					portrait_bedouin_clothing_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}

	bedouin_high_nobles = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = clothes
				template = bedouin_high_nobility_clothes
				range = { 0 1 } # For the randomness to work correctly
			}
			morph = {
				mode = modify_multiply
				gene = gene_bs_bust
				value = 0.8 # Setting this lower to reduce the exaggerated effect of large bust size when wearing clothes
				template = bust_clothes
			}

		}		
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				exists = court_owner
				court_owner = {
					has_royal_court = yes
					amenity_level = { type = court_fashion value = 5 }
				}
				portrait_bedouin_clothing_trigger = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				trigger_if = {
					limit = { exists = this }
					OR = {
						AND = {
							OR = {
								highest_held_title_tier > tier_county
								AND = {
									has_government = republic_government
									primary_title.tier > 1
								}
								AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
							}
							NOT = { AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = mercenary_government  } } # Blocked for mercenaries
							portrait_bedouin_clothing_trigger = yes
						}
						AND = {
							exists = primary_spouse
							OR = {
								is_ruler = no
								highest_held_title_tier = tier_barony
							}
							primary_spouse = {
								OR = {
									highest_held_title_tier > tier_county
									AND = {
										has_government = republic_government
										primary_title.tier > 1
									}
									AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
								}
								portrait_bedouin_clothing_spouse_trigger = yes
							}
						}
					}
				}
				trigger_else = {
					scope:highest_held_title_tier > tier_county
					portrait_bedouin_clothing_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}

	bedouin_war = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = clothes
				template = bedouin_war_nobility_clothes
				range = { 0 1 } # For the randomness to work correctly
			}
			morph = {
				mode = modify_multiply
				gene = gene_bs_bust
				value = 0.8 # Setting this lower to reduce the exaggerated effect of large bust size when wearing clothes
				template = bust_clothes
			}

		}
		outfit_tags = { military_outfit }
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 101
				portrait_wear_armor_trigger = yes
				portrait_bedouin_clothing_trigger = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 21		# So priests who are actively serving in a raised army as a commander or knight wear armor instead of priest robes
				portrait_wear_armor_currently_in_army_trigger = yes
			}
		}
	}

								AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
							}
							NOT = { AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = mercenary_government  } } # Blocked for mercenaries
							portrait_magyar_clothing_trigger = yes
						}
						AND = {
							exists = primary_spouse
							OR = {
								is_ruler = no
								highest_held_title_tier = tier_barony
							}
							primary_spouse = {
								OR = {
									highest_held_title_tier > tier_county
									AND = {
									has_government = republic_government
										primary_title.tier > 1
									}
									AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
								}
								portrait_magyar_clothing_spouse_trigger = yes
							}
						}
					}	
				}
				trigger_else = {
					scope:highest_held_title_tier > tier_county
					portrait_magyar_clothing_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}

	magyar_war = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = clothes_2
				template = magyar_war_nobility_clothes
				range = { 0 1 } # For the randomness to work correctly
			}
			morph = {
				mode = modify_multiply
				gene = gene_bs_bust
				value = 1.0 # Setting this lower to reduce the exaggerated effect of large bust size when wearing clothes
				template = bust_clothes
			}

		}	
		outfit_tags = { military_outfit }
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 101
				portrait_wear_armor_trigger = yes
				portrait_magyar_clothing_trigger = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 21		# So priests who are actively serving in a raised army as a commander or knight wear armor instead of priest robes
				portrait_wear_armor_currently_in_army_trigger = yes
			}
		}
	}

						        portrait_christian_iranian_trigger = yes		
							}
						}
					}
				}
				trigger_else = {
					scope:highest_held_title_tier > tier_county
				    portrait_early_eastern_iranian_trigger = yes
				    portrait_christian_iranian_trigger = yes	
				}
			}
		}
	}

			}
		}

	hindu_iranian_commoners = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = clothes
				template = indian_commoner_clothes
				range = { 0 1 } # For the randomness to work correctly
			}
			morph = {
				mode = modify_multiply
				gene = gene_bs_bust
				value = 0.8 # Setting this lower to reduce the exaggerated effect of large bust size when wearing clothes
				template = bust_clothes_light
			}

		}	
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 40
				scope:highest_held_title_tier < tier_county
				trigger_if = {
					limit = { exists = this }
					NOR = {
						AND = {
							exists = primary_spouse
							primary_spouse = {
								highest_held_title_tier > tier_county
							}
						}
						has_government = republic_government
						AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
					}
				}
				portrait_early_eastern_iranian_trigger = yes
				portrait_hindu_iranian_trigger = yes	
			}
		}
	}

	hindu_iranian_low_nobles = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = clothes
				template = indian_low_nobility_clothes
				range = { 0 1 } # For the randomness to work correctly
			}
			morph = {
				mode = modify_multiply
				gene = gene_bs_bust
				value = 0.8 # Setting this lower to reduce the exaggerated effect of large bust size when wearing clothes
				template = bust_clothes_light
			}

		}
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				exists = court_owner
				court_owner = {
					has_royal_court = yes
					amenity_level = { type = court_fashion value >= 4 }
				}
				portrait_early_eastern_iranian_trigger = yes
				portrait_hindu_iranian_trigger = yes	
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				trigger_if = {
					limit = { exists = this }
					OR = {
						AND = {
							highest_held_title_tier = tier_county
							NOR = {
								# has_dynasty = no
								AND = {
									has_government = republic_government
									primary_title.tier > 1
								}
								AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
							}
				            portrait_early_eastern_iranian_trigger = yes
				            portrait_hindu_iranian_trigger = yes	
						}
						AND = {
							exists = primary_spouse
							OR = {
								is_ruler = no
								highest_held_title_tier = tier_barony
							}
							primary_spouse = {
								highest_held_title_tier = tier_county
								NOR = {
									# has_dynasty = no
									AND = {
										has_government = republic_government
										primary_title.tier > 1
									}
									AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
								}
					            portrait_early_eastern_iranian_spouse_trigger = yes	
						        portrait_hindu_iranian_trigger = yes		
							}
						}
					}
				}
				trigger_else = {
					scope:highest_held_title_tier = tier_county
				 portrait_early_eastern_iranian_trigger = yes
				 portrait_hindu_iranian_trigger = yes	
				}

			}
		}
	}

	hindu_iranian_high_nobles = {

			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = clothes
				template = indian_high_nobility_clothes
				range = { 0 1 } # For the randomness to work correctly
			}
			morph = {
				mode = modify_multiply
				gene = gene_bs_bust
				value = 0.8 # Setting this lower to reduce the exaggerated effect of large bust size when wearing clothes
				template = bust_clothes_light
			}

		}		
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				exists = court_owner
				court_owner = {
					has_royal_court = yes
					amenity_level = { type = court_fashion value = 5 }
				}
				portrait_early_eastern_iranian_trigger = yes
				portrait_hindu_iranian_trigger = yes	
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				trigger_if = {
					limit = { exists = this }
					OR = {
						AND = {
							OR = {
								highest_held_title_tier > tier_county
								AND = {
									has_government = republic_government
									primary_title.tier > 1
								}
								AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
							}
							NOT = { AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = mercenary_government  } } # Blocked for mercenaries
				            portrait_early_eastern_iranian_trigger = yes
				            portrait_hindu_iranian_trigger = yes	
						}
						AND = {
							exists = primary_spouse
							OR = {
								is_ruler = no
								highest_held_title_tier = tier_barony
							}
							primary_spouse = {
								OR = {
									highest_held_title_tier > tier_county
									AND = {
										has_government = republic_government
										primary_title.tier > 1
									}
									AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
								}
					            portrait_early_eastern_iranian_spouse_trigger = yes	
						        portrait_hindu_iranian_trigger = yes		
							}
						}
					}
				}
				trigger_else = {
					scope:highest_held_title_tier > tier_county
				    portrait_early_eastern_iranian_trigger = yes
				    portrait_hindu_iranian_trigger = yes	
				}
			}
		}
	}

	andalusian_commoners = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = clothes
				template = fp2_muslim_commoner_clothes
				range = { 0.76 1 } # For the randomness to work correctly		
			}
			morph = {
				mode = modify_multiply
				gene = gene_bs_bust
				value = 1.0
				template = bust_clothes
			}

		}	
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 101
				scope:highest_held_title_tier < tier_county
				trigger_if = {
					limit = { exists = this }
					NOR = {
						AND = {
							exists = primary_spouse
							primary_spouse = {
								highest_held_title_tier > tier_county
							}
						}
						AND = {
							has_government = republic_government
							primary_title.tier > 1
						}
						AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
					}
				}
                OR = {
				AND = {
			    portrait_andalusian_clothing_trigger = yes
				portrait_iberian_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes
				     }					
				AND = {		
				portrait_iberia_muslim_clothing_trigger = yes
				portrait_iberia_muslim_trigger = yes
				portrait_iberian_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes							
				     }						
				  }
			}
		}
	}

	andalusian_low_nobles = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = clothes
				template = fp2_muslim_low_nobility_clothes
				range = { 0.61 1 } # For the randomness to work correctly		
			}
			morph = {
				mode = modify_multiply
				gene = gene_bs_bust
				value = 1.0
				template = bust_clothes
			}

		}	
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 101
				exists = court_owner
				court_owner = {
					has_royal_court = yes
					amenity_level = { type = court_fashion value >= 4 }
				}
                OR = {
				AND = {
			    portrait_andalusian_clothing_trigger = yes
				portrait_iberian_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes
				     }					
				AND = {		
				portrait_iberia_muslim_clothing_trigger = yes
				portrait_iberia_muslim_trigger = yes
				portrait_iberian_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes							
				     }						
				  }
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 101
				trigger_if = {
					limit = { exists = this }
					OR = {
						AND = {
							highest_held_title_tier = tier_county
							NOR = {
								# has_dynasty = no
								AND = {
									has_government = republic_government
									primary_title.tier > 1
								}
								AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
							}
                            OR = {
				             AND = {
			                portrait_andalusian_clothing_trigger = yes
				            portrait_iberian_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes
				               }					
				             AND = {		
				            portrait_iberia_muslim_clothing_trigger = yes
				            portrait_iberia_muslim_trigger = yes
				            portrait_iberian_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes							
				                 }						
				             }							
						}
						AND = {
							exists = primary_spouse
							OR = {
								is_ruler = no
								highest_held_title_tier = tier_barony
							}
							primary_spouse = {
								highest_held_title_tier = tier_county
								NOR = {
									# has_dynasty = no
									AND = {
									has_government = republic_government
										primary_title.tier > 1
									}
									AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
								}
								OR = {
				                  AND = {
			                     portrait_andalusian_clothing_spouse_trigger = yes
				                 portrait_iberian_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes
				                       }					
				                  AND = {		
				                 portrait_iberia_muslim_clothing_spouse_trigger = yes
				                 portrait_iberia_muslim_trigger = yes
				                 portrait_iberian_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes							
				                     }						
				                 }	
							}
						}
					}
				}
				trigger_else = {
					scope:highest_held_title_tier = tier_county
                    OR = {
				     AND = {
			        portrait_andalusian_clothing_trigger = yes
				    portrait_iberian_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes
				        }					
				     AND = {		
				    portrait_iberia_muslim_clothing_trigger = yes
				    portrait_iberia_muslim_trigger = yes
				    portrait_iberian_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes							
				       }						
				     }					
				}
			}
		}
	}

			modifier = {
				add = 50
				exists = court_owner
				court_owner = {
					has_royal_court = yes
					amenity_level = { type = court_fashion value = 5 }
				}
                OR = {
				AND = {
			    portrait_andalusian_clothing_trigger = yes
				NOT = { portrait_iberian_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes }
				     }					
				AND = {		
				portrait_iberia_muslim_clothing_trigger = yes
				portrait_iberia_muslim_trigger = yes
				NOT = { portrait_iberian_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes }							
				     }						
				  }
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				trigger_if = {
					limit = { exists = this }
					OR = {
						AND = {
							OR = {
								highest_held_title_tier > tier_county
								AND = {
									has_government = republic_government
									primary_title.tier > 1
								}
								AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
							}
							NOT = { AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = mercenary_government  } } # Blocked for mercenaries
                            OR = {
				             AND = {
			                portrait_andalusian_clothing_trigger = yes
				            NOT = { portrait_iberian_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes }
				               }					
				             AND = {		
				            portrait_iberia_muslim_clothing_trigger = yes
				            portrait_iberia_muslim_trigger = yes
				            NOT = { portrait_iberian_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes }							
				                 }						
				             }
						}
						AND = {
							exists = primary_spouse
							OR = {
								is_ruler = no
								highest_held_title_tier = tier_barony
							}
							primary_spouse = {
								OR = {
									highest_held_title_tier > tier_county
									AND = {
										has_government = republic_government
										primary_title.tier > 1
									}
									AND = { is_ruler = yes has_government = theocracy_government }
								}
								OR = {
				                  AND = {
			                     portrait_andalusian_clothing_spouse_trigger = yes
				                 NOT = { portrait_iberian_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes }
				                       }					
				                  AND = {		
				                 portrait_iberia_muslim_clothing_spouse_trigger = yes
				                 portrait_iberia_muslim_trigger = yes
				                 NOT = { portrait_iberian_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes }							
				                     }						
				                 }	
							}
						}
					}
				}
				trigger_else = {
					scope:highest_held_title_tier > tier_county
                    OR = {
				     AND = {
			        portrait_andalusian_clothing_trigger = yes
				    NOT = { portrait_iberian_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes }
				        }					
				     AND = {		
				    portrait_iberia_muslim_clothing_trigger = yes
				    portrait_iberia_muslim_trigger = yes
				    NOT = { portrait_iberian_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes }							
				       }						
				     }	
				}
			}
		}
	}

	andalusian_war_non_dlc = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = clothes
				template = fp2_muslim_war_nobility_clothes

			}
			morph = {

				gene = gene_bs_bust
				value = 1.0
				template = bust_clothes
			}

		}
		outfit_tags = { military_outfit }
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 101
				portrait_wear_armor_trigger = yes
                OR = {
				AND = {
			    portrait_andalusian_clothing_trigger = yes
				NOT = { portrait_iberian_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes }
				     }					
				AND = {		
				portrait_iberia_muslim_clothing_trigger = yes
				portrait_iberia_muslim_trigger = yes
				NOT = { portrait_iberian_flavor_dlc_trigger = yes }							
				     }						
				  }			
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 21		# So priests who are actively serving in a raised army as a commander or knight wear armor instead of priest robes
				portrait_wear_armor_currently_in_army_trigger = yes
			}
		}
